--- GOAL PLAN ---
rewritten_query: What type of individual life insurance policy is issued when converting a term policy?
reasoning: The query asks about the type of individual policy that is issued upon conversion. This is a specific insurance-related question, likely referring to life insurance policies. The term "conversion" in insurance typically refers to the process of converting a term life insurance policy into a permanent policy (such as whole life or universal life) without requiring additional underwriting. The key here is identifying the type of policy that results from this conversion. The query is focused on a single, well-defined fact: the policy type issued after conversion. No chaining of facts is needed—this is a straightforward lookup.
